Evaluation dataset tables

AndroidWorld-derived task specifications and recorded fixture observations. No agent outcomes are included.

Evaluation buckets 9 rows · source: evaluation_buckets.csv

Bucket IdQuestionGenerated Reference PairsIndependent VerifierAndroidworld Source MaterialStatus
record_integrityAre target records present exactly once and do all requested fields match?150SQLite/provider/file exact target predicate plus protected-state diff; accessibility/screenshot confirms discoverabilityCalendar, Expense, Recipe SQLite tasks; Markor files; Contacts providerreference_pairs_and_predicate_generated; existing_SQLite_device_fixture
persistence_lifecycleDoes the accepted state survive the lifecycle boundary the user expects?60canonical store snapshot before/after boundary; compare exact target and protected rowsCalendar, Expense, Recipe, Markor task setup/evaluatorsreference_pairs_and_predicate_generated; live_fixture_hook_pending
action_effect_side_effectsDid the action take effect exactly once on the intended object without collateral change?60before/after exact state diff with target delta and protected-state invariantsSQLite add/delete, file move/delete, Contacts/SMS, system settingsreference_pairs_and_predicate_generated; live_fixture_hook_pending
navigation_transitionsDid the agent reach the required screen and handle back/cancel/search/return paths correctly?60expected activity/accessibility-tree state sequence plus independent final store statemulti-step Calendar/Expense/Recipe/Markor/composite workflowsreference_pairs_and_predicate_generated; live_fixture_hook_pending
control_reachabilityAre required controls visible, enabled, clickable, and on screen?60UI Automator/accessibility properties and bounds; safe hit-test for non-destructive controltask screens and screen variation profilesreference_pairs_and_predicate_generated; live_fixture_hook_pending
visual_layoutAre labels and controls legible, in bounds, and free of harmful overlap or clipping?60geometry from UI bounds plus screenshot/OCR evidence; blinded human adjudication for ambiguityAndroidWorld screen-variation configurations and data-entry screensreference_pairs_and_predicate_generated; live_fixture_hook_pending
presentation_consistencyDoes displayed text accurately represent stored values, units, and required formatting?60compare DB/provider value with accessibility text and OCR/screenshot; deterministic format predicateExpense, Calendar, Recipe recordsreference_pairs_and_predicate_generated; live_fixture_hook_pending
permission_stateDoes the app's behavior match the granted or denied Android runtime permission state?30dumpsys package and AppOps state plus deterministic app behavior checkAndroidWorld permission/system tasksreference_pairs_and_predicate_generated; live_fixture_hook_pending
success_signal_truthDoes success feedback agree with the durable result?60independent durable-state oracle plus timestamped toast/screen exposure recordAndroidWorld add-record templates supply workflows/values, not deceptive signalsreference_pairs_and_predicate_generated; live_fixture_hook_pending
